| `WA_Research_Basic.description` | Learn the basics of Japanese technology. We will be able to study various Japanese technologies. | Learn the foundations of Japanese techniques to unlock further research. | 学习基础日本技术。解锁各种日本技术研究。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Basic.label` | Building Basic | Basic architecture | 和风建筑技术_基础 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Basic.description` | Learn the basics of Japanese style building Technology.We will be able to build basic walls and fences | Learn basic Japanese building techniques to construct simple walls and fences. | 研究和风建筑的基础。能建筑基本的墙壁与围栏。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Advanced.label` | Building Advanced | Advanced architecture | 和风建筑技术_应用 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Advanced.description` | Learn the application of Japanese style building Technology.We will be able to build various walls and fences. | Learn advanced Japanese building techniques to construct a wider range of walls and fences. | 研究和风建筑的进阶技术。以解锁各式墙壁与围栏。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Castle.label` | Building Castle | Fortified architecture | 和风筑城技术 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Castle.description` | Learn the Japanese style casting technology.We will be able to build Robust building for defense. | Learn Japanese fortification techniques to build strong defensive structures. | 研究和风筑城技术。适用建造于防卫的坚牢建筑。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Garden.label` | Gardening | Gardening | 和风造园技术 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Garden.description` | Learn the Japanese style gardening technology.We will be able to build the landscape. | Learn Japanese gardening techniques to shape the landscape. | 研究和风造园技术。能解锁用以美化景观的建筑。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Funiture.label` | Funiture | Furniture | 和风家具技术 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Funiture.description` | Learn the Japanese style funiture technology.We will be able to build various furniture. | Learn to make Japanese-style furniture. | 研究制作和风家具的方法。能制造多种家具。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Amusement.label` | Amusement | Amusement | 和风娱乐 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Amusement.description` | Learn the Japanese style Entertainment technology.We will be able to build some entertainment supplies. | Learn to make equipment for traditional Japanese pastimes. | 学习娱乐技术。以制造多种娱乐用品。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Art.label` | Art | Art | 和风艺术品 |  |
| `WA_Research_Art.description` | Learn art.You will be able to make beautiful art pieces. | Learn to create beautiful works of art. | 学习艺术。能制作美丽的艺术品。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Farming.label` | Farming | Farming | 和风植物 |  |
| `WA_Research_Farming.description` | Learn cultivation of plants indispensable for Japanese style living. | Learn cultivation of plants indispensable for Japanese style living. | 学习如何栽培日常生活中不可缺乏的植物。 |  |
| `WA_Research_TeaTimeset.label` | Tea Time | Tea Time | 茶会 |  |
| `WA_Research_TeaTimeset.description` | Learn how to make tea.We will be able to make tea and kinds of tea confectionery. | Learn to prepare tea and traditional sweets to accompany it. | 学习如何泡茶。并能制作多种茶类与茶点。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Dressing.label` | Dress up | Dress up | 和服 |  |
| `WA_Research_Dressing.description` | Learn how to make daily clothing. | Learn how to make daily clothing. | 学习日常用服装的制作手法。 |  |

## ChineseSimplified/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-Amusement.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseSimplified | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_Koto.label` | Koto | Koto | 筝 |  |
| `WA_Koto.description` | Koto is a thirteen-stringed musical instrumentsimilar to a zither.The stirngs stretch over an oblong board. | A Japanese thirteen-stringed zither with strings stretched over a long wooden body. | 日本筝是有十三根琴弦的弦楽器。琴弦平行琴身。 |  |
| `WA_Karuta.label` | Karuta | Karuta | 歌牌 |  |
| `WA_Karuta.description` | Karuta is an indoor playground equipment that uses cards, mainly playing in the New Year. | A traditional Japanese card game, especially popular at New Year. | 歌牌是在正月时游玩，利用卡牌进行的室内娱乐道具。 |  |
| `WA_ShellMatching.label` | ShellMatching | Shell matching | 贝合 |  |
| `WA_ShellMatching.description` | Shell Matching is a Japanese game transmitted from the Heian period. It was a play of aristocrats who competed for coloring of seashells, beauty of shape, rarity, or singing songs with the shellfish as a theme and competing for superiority or inferiority. | A traditional game of matching shells, recalling courtly pastimes in which their shapes, colours and associated poetry were admired. | 贝合是传自于平安时代的日本游戏。内容包括互相比较贝殻的色泽与外型，以及珍稀度。并以手中的贝壳为题材行歌颂咏，以分高下。是流行于贵族中的游戏。 |  |
| `WA_Sugoroku.label` | Sugoroku | Sugoroku | 双陆 |  |
| `WA_Sugoroku.description` | Sugoroku is a board game that advances the piece in the square according to the eyes that waved the dice and brings it closer. | A board game in which players move their pieces according to dice rolls. | 双陆棋是种桌上游戏。棋子的移动以掷骰子的点数决定，首位把所有马移离棋盘的玩者可获得胜利。 |  |
| `WA_Cassolette.label` | Cassolette | Incense burner | 香炉 |  |
| `WA_Cassolette.description` | An Cassolette is used to heat a perfume in a solid state and emit fragrance components.The nobility of the Heian period was a routine thing for men and women to be able to wear kimonos into incense burners and smell their bodies and clothes with scents. | A vessel for heating solid incense to release its scent. At the Heian court, incense was also used to perfume clothing. | 香炉是用于加热固体香料，藉以散发熏香的容器。平安时代的贵族，无论男女皆会在穿着和服前会先过香，使熏芳环绕其身。 |  |

| `WA_Fusuma01_Akebono.description` | A sliding partition of paper or cloth stretched over a wooden frame. Its fragile paper is easily punctured. | A light wooden lattice covered with translucent paper. It admits soft light while screening the view. Its fragile paper is easily punctured. | 障子是由薄纸覆在格状骨架上而成，具有少许透光性。轻而方便开关，但耐久度也不高。现今的障子，论其型式，可回溯至鎌仓幕府以前，平清盛的时代。具有十分易破的构造，被宠物及儿童开了个洞也是日常茶饭之事。 |  |

| `WA_Dressing_Princess.description` | Kimono of Princess.Princess' s dignity encourages surrounding friends. | A princess's kimono. Her dignity encourages nearby allies. | 皇室、公卿、将军，以及大名等显贵人家的女儿。公主高雅的品格能激励周围的友方角色。 |  |
| `WA_Dressing_Sumoman.label` | Kimono-Rikishi | Kimono-Rikishi | 和服-力士 |  |
| `WA_Dressing_Sumoman.description` | Kimono of Rikishi.A Yokozuna with a mighty power overwhelms the enemy. | A wrestler's kimono. The yokozuna's imposing presence intimidates enemies. | 力士的服装。力大无穷的横纲所散发出的魄力足以压制敌对角色。 |  |

## ChineseSimplified/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-FarmPlant.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseSimplified | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_FarmPlant_Azuki.label` | Azuki | Azuki | 红豆 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Azuki.description` | Tie the dark red beans to the sheath.<br>Beans are cooked or boiled sweet and then made into bean paste. | A plant bearing pods of dark red beans. These can be cooked as food or sweetened into bean paste. | 暗红色的豆粒藏于豆荚中。<br>煮熟的红豆能作为料理食用，加糖慢熬则能做成内馅。 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_TeaTree.label` | TeaTree | TeaTree | 茶树 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_TeaTree.description` | It is said that cultivation began in China since BC, it is used not only for edible but also for appreciation.<br>The tea extracted from the leaves with water is loved around the world. | A shrub long cultivated for its leaves, which can be infused to make tea. | 据说在公元前就已在中国开始栽培，不单作为饮品，作为鉴赏植物亦有一分雅致。经水冲泡而制的茶受到世界各地喜爱。 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Igusa.label` | Rush | Rush | 灯心草 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Igusa.description` | It is an elongated plant inhabited by wetlands and large ones grow to about 1.6 m.<br>It is mainly used as a raw material for tatami mats, but it was used as a medicinal herbs in the past. | A tall wetland rush used primarily to make tatami mats, and historically in some herbal remedies. | 在湿地生长的细长植物，长者可达1.6米。常用于制作榻榻米，古时也常用作药草。 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Kouzo.label` | Kouzo | Kouzo | 楮树 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Kouzo.description` | In spring, light yellow green flowers bloom and red fruits in early summer.<br>It is used as a raw material for paper because it can take high quality fibers from the bark. | A paper-mulberry tree whose bark provides strong fibres for papermaking. | 楮树会在春天绽放淡黄绿色的花，立夏时则会结下红色的果实。能从树皮抽取良好的纤维，常作为和纸的原料。 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Kuwa.label` | Kuwa | Kuwa | 桑树 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Kuwa.description` | Mulberry ties the purple of purplish red purple.<br>It is used as a tree for silkworms since ancient times, it is a plant indispensable for making silk. | A mulberry tree with purplish-red fruit. Its leaves feed silkworms, making it important for silk production. | 桑树会结下红紫的野苺。自古桑叶就常作为丝蚕的饲料，是制作细绢时不可或缺的植物。 |  |

## ChineseSimplified/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-FoldingScreen.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseSimplified | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_FoldingScreen.label` | Folding Screen | Folding Screen | 屏风 |  |
| `WA_FoldingScreen.description` | Byobu is a folding screen which consists of a wooden frame covered with thick Japanese paper or cloth, usually silk.<br>It is used as a decorative screen or room devider. | A folding screen of wooden frames covered with thick Japanese paper or cloth, often silk. It decorates a room or divides the space. | 屏风在西方又名折幕。以厚层和纸及绢丝覆盖木架而成。屏风通常陈设于室内显著位置，起到分隔、美化、挡风、协调等作用。 |  |

## ChineseSimplified/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-Food.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseSimplified | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_GreenTea.label` | Green Tea | Green Tea | 抹茶 |  |
| `WA_GreenTea.description` | Green Tea is a mixture of powdered tea leaves into hot water. | Green Tea is a mixture of powdered tea leaves into hot water. | 在日本，茶叶多被分类为绿茶，在日本时最为常见的饮品。茶会时则多制成抹茶，抹茶亦是绿茶的一种。将茶叶捣碎成末，加水搅拌而成。 |  |
| `WA_IchigoDaifuku.label` | Ichigo Daifuku | Ichigo Daifuku | 草苺大福 |  |
| `WA_IchigoDaifuku.description` | "Daifuku" is a rice cake stuffed with sweet filling like red bean paste.Daifuku literally means "great luck".<br>When eating, the fragrance of fresh strawberry spreads in the mouth, you can enjoy the sweetness of soft rice cake texture and elegant sweetness in exquisite balance. | A soft mochi filled with sweet red bean paste and a fresh strawberry. The fruit's tartness balances the sweet filling. | 大福一种是包着甜馅的糕饼。照其字义，有着象征「幸运」的含意。嚼下一口只觉苺香鲜甜奔放，柔软的饼皮与高雅的甜馅共谱出一章和谐。 |  |
| `WA_MitarashiDango.label` | Mitarashi Dango | Mitarashi Dango | 御手洗团子 |  |
| `WA_MitarashiDango.description` | "Mitarashi Dango" is a skewer dumpling with sugar and soy sauce.<br>The amber colored sweet taste is compatible with the rice flour dumplings, and the fragrant aroma caused by grilling with charcoal also enhances appetite. | Skewered rice-flour dumplings coated in a sweet soy-based sauce. Their grilled aroma is appetising. | 御手洗团子是淋上砂糖酱油的团子串。琥珀色的辛甜淋酱与糯米团子堪称绝配，再经炭火烘烤后香气四溢，令人食指大动。 |  |
| `WA_Geppei.label` | Geppei | Geppei | 月饼 |  |
| `WA_Geppei.description` | "Geppei" is an old sweet which is eaten in Mid-Autumn Festival in China.<br>It has a round shape that is regarded as a full moon, wrapped in a soft dough and baked red bean paste mixed with walnuts, pine nuts etc. in soft cloth. | A round mooncake filled with sweet bean paste and nuts, traditionally associated with the Mid-Autumn Festival. | 月饼常在中国的中秋节食用，是自古就有的甜点。取自满月的外形，以柔软的饼衣包覆含有胡桃及松果的红豆馅，长时烘焙而成。 |  |
| `WA_KashiwaMochi.label` | Kashiwa Mochi | Kashiwa Mochi | 柏饼 |  |
| `WA_KashiwaMochi.description` | "Kashihachi" is wrapped in red bean paste with rice cake of rice flour. | A rice-flour mochi filled with sweet bean paste and wrapped in an oak leaf. | 柏饼是以上新粉制成的饼衣，填入红豆馅，再用槲叶包裹而成的甜点。软呼呼又带嚼劲，咀嚼后，只觉糯米独特的柔和甜香在口中绽放。 |  |
| `WA_Kasuteira.label` | Kasuteira | Kasuteira | 蜂蜜蛋糕 |  |
| `WA_Kasuteira.description` | "Kasuteira" is confections adopted from Portugal.<br>However, the actual situation is a completely different sweet that developed independently in Japan.<br>Kasuteira is made by burning a flour fabric mixed with plenty of sugar and eggs. | A light cake made from eggs, flour and sugar. Inspired by Portuguese baking, it developed its own distinctive form in Japan. | 蜂蜜蛋糕是传自葡萄牙的南蛮糕点(日本古时称葡萄牙为"南蛮")，在日本长年发展后，现已独树一帜。加入大量砂糖与蛋制成的糕身，口感刚柔并具，扎实且松软。配上砂糖的香甜如同画龙点睛，成就奢华的逸品。 |  |
| `WA_Kintuba.label` | Kintuba | Kintuba | 金鹗饼 |  |
| `WA_Kintuba.description` | "Kintuba" is a sweet made with red bean paste hardened with agar and baked with water flavored flour. | Sweet bean paste set with agar and cooked with a thin coating of batter. | 先以寒天固定红豆馅，再涂上溶于水的小麦粉，烘制而成的糕点。色泽亮丽的粒馅，将红豆的风味发挥得淋漓尽致，每一口都能将鲜甜的滋味烙印在脑海。 |  |
| `WA_Nerikiri.label` | Nerikiri | Nerikiri | 练切 |  |
| `WA_Nerikiri.description` | "Nerikiri" is a confectionary refined and adjusted by adding a connecting ingredient to white bean paste. | A decorative sweet shaped from white bean paste mixed with a binder, often coloured and formed into delicate patterns. | 练切是在白馅中添入黏性食材，经过调整后反复捏制而成的生菓子。又因其典雅的外型，与富含层次的风味而被归类为「上生菓子」。练切的外型映照着四季的转移，紧紧捉住观赏者的心。 |  |
| `WA_SakuraNUguisu.label` | Sakura and Uguisu Mochi | Sakura and Uguisu Mochi | 樱饼与莺饼 |  |
| `WA_SakuraNUguisu.description` | It is a sweet imitating the color of cherry blossoms and the color of young plants. | Sweets evoking cherry blossoms and fresh spring shoots. | 以歌咏春季的樱色及翠色为主题的糕点。两者皆以米制饼皮包覆馅儿而成。樱饼轻柔的樱叶幽香，配上莺饼的豆粉甜香，是幸福的芬芳。 |  |
| `WA_Youkan.label` | Youkan | Youkan | 羊羹 |  |
| `WA_Youkan.description` | "Youkan" is a Japanese sweet made by pouring azuki bean paste in mold and solidified with agar. | A Japanese sweet made from azuki bean paste, moulded and set with agar. | 羊羹是将红豆馅放置于模子中，倒入寒天后凝固而成的和菓子。糖分高的羊羹在被切开时会流出砂糖，因此放置一段时间后会在外侧形成一层白霜。<br>  白霜的颗粒感与羊羹内馅的软嫩口感，谱成一章甜美的协奏曲。 |  |

| `WA_HFuton01m.description` | Futon is a thick bed quilt or mattress stuffed with cottonwool.A set of futon placed on Tatami mats is used as bedding.This futon is highly antimicrobial treated and can be used in medical practice. | A futon with an antimicrobial treatment, suitable for medical care. | 布团是以填入厚重的棉花以制成的床垫及棉被套件。铺在榻榻米上的一组布团能作为寝具使用。这个布团经过抗菌处理，能在医疗手术时派上用场。 |  |

## ChineseSimplified/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-Glow.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseSimplified | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_Glow_01Toudai.label` | Cow dung lighthouse | Oil lamp stand | 牛粪灯台 |  |
| `WA_Glow_01Toudai.description` | The lighthouse lights the light with a light on the lamp core soaked in "kerosene" produced from the animal's oil.<br>It is said that it began to be used around the Heian era, and it is said that the aristocratic group used to love mainly.<br>Cow dung lighthouse has been called by this name because its base part resembles the form of cow dung. | An oil lamp with a wick resting in a shallow dish. Its traditional name refers to the shape of its base, which resembles cow dung. Such lamps were once used in aristocratic households. | 灯台，是将灯芯浸入由动物的脂肪制成的「灯油」中，用以点火照明的器具。据说从平安时代就有使用纪录，主要是作为贵族圈的爱用品。其名源自于灯座形状相似牛粪，故作此称。 |  |
| `WA_Glow_02Gatou.label` | Gato | Gato | 瓦灯 |  |
| `WA_Glow_02Gatou.description` | Gato is also a lighting fixture made from "kerosene" made from animal fat.<br>It began to be used around the Muromachi period, and it circulated enough to be used as a light source for the common people in the Edo period.<br>It is called a tile light because it makes it by baking it using the same soil as the Kawara.<br>It is one of the popular reasons to be flame retardant materials. | A clay oil lamp made from material similar to roof tiles. Its fire-resistant construction helped it become a common household light. | 瓦灯是以动物脂肪所制成的「灯油」为燃料的照明器具。从室町时代就传用至今，江户时代时作为寻常百姓的生活光源而被广泛利用。如同烧制瓦砖一般，透过窑烧黏土而成，其名亦源于此。不易燃的特性也利于人所用。 |  |
| `WA_Glow_03Andon.label` | Ariake lantern | Ariake lantern | 有明行灯 |  |
| `WA_Glow_03Andon.description` | Lighthouses and Gato were problematic in windshields, so lanterns wrapped in Japanese paper were invented.<br>Lanterns were originally used for carrying, but since it was "kerosene" made from animal oil, there is a difficulty in portability and it will be used for indoor stationary use.<br>Ariake lanterns can be illuminated brightly when used out from the box, and when used in a box it gained popularity as an excellent one that can be used as a bedtime light with suppressed brightness. | A paper-covered lantern that shelters its flame. Outside its box it gives a brighter light; inside, it becomes a subdued night light. | 因为灯台与瓦灯防风不佳，易被吹灭。以和纸框住火光的「行灯」因而问世。行灯本是作为行路时的手提式光源。又因以动物脂肪制成的「灯油」常于行进时液出而不易携带，现在多摆设于室内使用。有明行灯可从箱中取出，增加室内照明效果。就寝时则能放入箱中，降低明亮度。因其优秀的机能性而颇具人气。 |  |
| `WA_Glow_04Bonbori.label` | Bonbori | Bonbori | 雪洞 |  |
| `WA_Glow_04Bonbori.description` | The weak point of kerosene which is liquid is easy to spill and lack portability.<br>So lighting using candles that will not spill will be accepted.<br>Snow cave is made by covering a candle on a candlestick with paper or cloth, and something of various shapes such as square, hexagon, octagon, round shape etc are made.<br>Even now there are habits left to decorate Bonbori during events such as dolls' festival. | A lantern made by covering a frame with paper or cloth around a candle. Bonbori still decorate traditional celebrations such as the Doll Festival. | 液态灯油液在行路携带时有着容易溅出的缺点。基于这点，固态蜡烛开始广用于照明灯具。雪洞是将蜡烛放置在烛台上，再以和纸或布来包覆制成的物品。能制成四角形、六角形、八角形、圆型等多种造型。在现今，雏祭等节日时依然会以雪洞作为装饰使用。 |  |
| `WA_Glow_05Chouchin.label` | Chochin | Chochin | 提灯 |  |
| `WA_Glow_05Chouchin.description` | Chochin is also lighting with a candle, which is very light because it covers the body made of bamboo chicken and Japanese paper.<br>It became a structure that can be folded up, and because of its portability it became an alternative to lighting for portability.<br>It is said that it was made in the Azuchi-Momoyama period and spread in the Edo period.<br>Furthermore, even now the contents are different and loved and it continues to be used. | A lightweight paper lantern stretched over a folding bamboo frame. Portable and compact, it has long been used in streets and at festivals. | 提灯亦是利用蜡烛的照明用具。用竹板与和纸制成的骨架十分轻便，是其一大特征。加上可折迭的机能，使其携带性优于行灯而逐渐代之。最早始于安土桃山时代，在江戸时代开始普及。即使是现今，亦能放入多不同光源而被持续使用。 |  |
| `WA_Glow_06Bonfire.label` | Bonfire | Bonfire | 篝火 |  |
| `WA_Glow_06Bonfire.description` | Bonfire are one of the lighting fixtures since ancient times, mainly installed outdoors.<br>Put the fire in a steel basket with fuel like pine as a fuel.<br>In addition to being used for outdoor events such as martial forces and festivals, it also fulfills the role of a fishing light on fishing boats.<br>Even now it is used at night festival events and cormorants. | An outdoor fire held in a metal basket. It lights gatherings, festivals and some fishing boats. | 篝火は是以古以来就有的照明用具。主要放置于屋外，以松木等富含树脂的树木作为燃料，放入铁制笼座中点火使用。在排演军阵、庆祝节日等屋外活动时常被使用，此外，在渔船上亦能作为聚鱼灯。现今则能在夜间祭典上，或是鸬鹚捕鱼时派上用场。 |  |
| `WA_Glow_07LongBrazier.label` | Brazier | Brazier | 火钵 |  |
| `WA_Glow_07LongBrazier.description` | Brazier is half-filled with burning charcoal.<br>It is one of the traditional heating devices.<br>In addition to heating, you can boil tea or tobacco pipe. | A vessel partly filled with glowing charcoal. It provides warmth and can heat tea or light a pipe. | 火钵是在陶器中，放入半分满的木炭，燃烧以提升室内温度。火钵是日本传统的暖房器具之一。 |  |

## ChineseSimplified/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-Irori.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseSimplified | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_Irori.label` | Irori | Irori | 围炉里 |  |
| `WA_Irori.description` | It is a type of furnace permanently installed indoors.<br>It is a corner established in traditional Japanese houses to cut off the floor square, open ash, spread firewood and charcoal fire.<br>It is of the type put in the middle of a large room and has various roles such as heating, cooking, lighting, drying. | A sunken hearth in a square opening in the floor, filled with ash, wood and charcoal. It provides heating, cooking, light and a place to dry things. | 一种设置于屋内的永久性家俱。传统的日式住宅会在地板挖开一块四方形空间并铺上灰烬，用来燃烧木炭或柴火。设置在主厅房的围炉里，具有暖房、调理、照明、干燥等多种用途。 |  |
| `WA_Chagama.label` | Irori(TeaSet) | Irori(TeaSet) | 茶室用围炉里 |  |
| `WA_Chagama.description` | It is a type of furnace permanently installed indoors.<br>It is a corner established in traditional Japanese houses to cut off the floor square, open ash, spread firewood and charcoal fire.<br>Most of the hearths used in the tea room are sized for one tatami mat, mainly used for boiling water. | A sunken hearth set into the floor of a tea room, primarily used to heat water for tea. | 一种设置于屋内的永久性家俱。传统的日式住宅会在地板挖开一块四方形空间并铺上灰烬，用来燃烧木炭或柴火。<br>用于茶室的围炉里通常占一张榻榻米大小，主要用来煮开水。 |  |

| `WA_Igusa.label` | Rush | Rush | 灯心草 |  |
| `WA_Igusa.description` | Rush is mainly used as a raw material for tatami mats, but it was used as a medicinal herbs in the past. | Rush is mainly used as a raw material for tatami mats, but it was used as a medicinal herbs in the past. | 灯心草主要用于制作榻榻米，在古时亦作为药草使用。 |  |
| `RPC_Kouzo.label` | Kouzo | Kouzo | 楮树皮 |  |
| `RPC_Kouzo.description` | It is a bundle of bark which took from Kouzo Tree.<br>Fibers of Kouzo bark are strongly intertwined long.<br>Paper made from Kouzo is strong and lasts long. | A bundle of paper-mulberry bark. Its long, intertwined fibres make strong, lasting paper. | 将树皮剥开后捆成一束的物品。楮树皮的纤维具有细长又相互纠结的特性，适用于制作高韧性而不易破的和纸。 |  |
| `RPC_Cocoon.label` | Cocoon | Cocoon | 茧 |  |
| `RPC_Cocoon.description` | It is the cocoon made by silkworm larvae.<br>Weaving the thread by spinning it will become the finest textile. | A silkworm cocoon. Its thread can be reeled off to produce fine silk fabric. | 丝蚕的幼虫制成的茧。经由纺丝编织便可成为最上等的布帛。 |  |

## ChineseSimplified/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-Wall.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseSimplified | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_Wall_Kawara.label` | Rampart | Rampart | 城壁 |  |
| `WA_Wall_Kawara.description` | It is a type of painted wall.<br>The roof tiles(Kawara) roofed on the plastics wall also combine rain removal and decoration.<br>At that time the tiles were expensive, mainly used for the outer walls of samurai residences. | A plastered wall capped with roof tiles for weather protection and decoration. Such walls once surrounded the residences of samurai. | 一种涂墙，涂墙是使用水、土、植物等天然素材作为原料的墙壁。铺上漆喰壁的屋瓦既能防雨，亦带有装饰效果。在当时的瓦砖价格不斐，主要用来建筑武家房屋的外墙。 |  |
| `WA_Wall_Demado.label` | Rampart(bay window) | Rampart(bay window) | 城壁(凸窗) |  |
| `WA_Wall_Demado.description` | It is one with a bay window attached to the wall with tiles.<br>The bay window functioned as a surveillance window besides the role of giving aesthetic appearance during normal times.<br>In addition, it fulfills the role of firearms and stone drops at wartime. | A tiled wall with a projecting window for watching the surroundings and supporting its defenders. | 在铺有屋瓦的墙壁上设置凸窗。凸窗平时除了美观效果，亦能用于监视外侧。战斗时则能作为铳眼、投石处来迎敌。 |  |
| `WA_Wall_StoneWall.label` | Stone Wall | Stone Wall | 石垣 |  |
| `WA_Wall_StoneWall.description` | Ishigaki is a wall created by assembling stones and rocks, and is mainly used as a defense such as a castle.<br>Initially it was just a stack of natural stones, but as the era has progressed, things that increased the defensibility by loading stones machined without gaps became mainstream. | Ishigaki is a wall created by assembling stones and rocks, and is mainly used as a defense such as a castle.<br>Initially it was just a stack of natural stones, but as the era has progressed, things that increased the defensibility by loading stones machined without gaps became mainstream. | 石垣是由石头或岩石堆砌而成的墙壁，只要用来建造城塞的防卫墙。在早期只是将普通的石头堆栈起来而已。随着时代演进，将加工后的石块无间隙地堆砌成墙，以提高防御性能的形式逐渐成为主流。 |  |
| `WA_Wall_WaterMoats.label` | Water Moats | Water Moats | 城壕 |  |
| `WA_Wall_WaterMoats.description` | The moat was dug around the castle and temple from the ancient times to prevent invasion of enemies and animals.<br>It has width and depth that can not easily be exceeded and it functions as an important defense facility.<br>Those water-trenched to the moat are called Mizuhori in particular, and have been incorporated into many castles since the modern era. | A wide, deep ditch dug around a fortification to slow intruders. Filled with water, it is a serious obstacle for attackers. | 是古时由人工挖凿，环绕整座城、宫城、寺院等主要建筑的壕沟，具有防御作用，可防止敌人或动物入侵。具有不易越过的深度及宽度，能作为重要的防卫建设使用。<br>  掘沟放水而成的城壕又称护城河，近代以后有多座城堡采用这种设计。 |  |
| `WA_Wall_DryMoats.label` | Dry Moats | Dry Moats | 干壕 |  |
| `WA_Wall_DryMoats.description` | The moat was dug around the castle and temple from the ancient times to prevent invasion of enemies and animals.<br>It has width and depth that can not easily be exceeded and it functions as an important defense facility.<br>Those water-trenched to the moat are called Mizuhori in particular, and have been incorporated into many castles since the modern era. | A wide, deep ditch dug around a fortification to slow intruders. It stays dry and is an obstacle for attackers. | 是古时由人工挖凿，环绕整座城、宫城、寺院等主要建筑的壕沟，具有防御作用，可防止敌人或动物入侵。具有不易越过的深度及宽度，能作为重要的防卫建设使用。<br>  掘沟而不引河而成的城壕又称干壕，中世纪时的壕沟多为干壕。空壕能防止敌人透过船只入侵，也没有护城河需顾虑到河面结冰的问题。 |  |
| `WA_Step.label` | Step | Step | 阶梯 |  |
| `WA_Step.description` | The staircase is a structure to move to a place with a height difference.<br>It is divided into several horizontal stages with a height that can be raised and lowered by human feet.<br>Rimworld without the notion of step difference has no meaning other than appearance. | A decorative step suggesting a change in floor level. RimWorld does not model such changes, so its role here is purely visual. | 楼梯是常用来联系具有高低差的两地时所采用的交通建设。以人类的双脚能在其上升降的多个水平段落来分割垂直高度。在边缘世界这款没有段差概念的游戏，除了当装饰以外并不具有其他意义。 |  |
| `WA_Gate_CastleGate.label` | Castle Gate | Castle Gate | 城门 |  |
| `WA_Gate_CastleGate.description` | The castle gate was one of the few places to enter the castle and it was one of the most important defense lines at wartime.<br>For that reason, it was easy to make a robust structure that can not be withdrawn, and a lot of ingenious efforts were made for interception. | A strong, fortified castle entrance designed to aid its defenders. | 城门是能进入城内的少数区域，也是战时最为重要的防线之一。因此，<br>  城门大多设计成无法轻易通过的坚固设施，凝聚了工匠的无数心力，以利于守城方迎击敌人。 |  |
| `WA_Gate_IronGate.label` | Castle Door | Castle Door | 铁扉 |  |
| `WA_Gate_IronGate.description` | The castle gate was one of the few places to enter the castle and it was one of the most important defense lines at wartime.<br>Some of the gate is made of heavy iron, and even just opening it required a very large effort. | A heavy door protecting a castle entrance, sometimes reinforced with iron. | 城门是能进入城内的少数区域，也是战时最为重要的防线之一。<br>  门扉的部分由沉重的铁所制，仅是要打开它就得耗上巨大劳力。 |  |
| `WA_Fence_Yamatobei.label` | Yamato Fence | Yamato Fence | 大和塀 |  |
| `WA_Fence_Yamatobei.description` | The fence is a workpiece that has the effect of preventing invasion of people and animals and blindfolding.<br>Yamato-Fence is a fence stretched from the front and back with alternating plates one by one.<br>I open a gap by passing a crosspiece between the boards.<br>By doing so, it is a simple fence that blocks the line of sight and also passes the wind. | A fence with boards alternating on either side of its crosspieces. It blocks the view while allowing air to pass. | 围栏是为了防止敌人与动物的入侵，且具有遮蔽效果的防卫工事。<br>大和塀是在一层木制栅栏，在里外具有数个重合部分。木板间能透过短桟开启缝隙。藉此形成能遮蔽视线，又能通风的简易屏障。 |  |
| `WA_Fence_Sukibei.label` | Suki Fence | Suki Fence | 透塀 |  |
| `WA_Fence_Sukibei.description` | The fence is a workpiece that has the effect of preventing invasion of people and animals and blindfolding.<br>Suki-Fence is a covered fence, so that the interior can be seen through by placing a lattice in the middle.<br>It is mainly used in shrines and temples. | A covered fence with a central lattice that allows a partial view inside. Often used at shrines and temples. | 围栏是为了防止敌人与动物的入侵，且具有遮蔽效果的防卫工事。<br>透塀是搭盖了屋顶的围栏，中间凿了数个方格，能从方格看到内部。主要使用于神社及寺庙。 |  |
| `WA_Fence_Tuijibei.label` | Tsuiji Fence | Tsuiji Fence | 筑地塀 |  |
| `WA_Fence_Tuijibei.description` | The fence is a workpiece that has the effect of preventing invasion of people and animals and blindfolding.<br>Tsuiji-Fence sets up a framework by setting up a pillar on the foundation of stone wall, pouring and kneading the kneading soil.<br>It has been used in public places and temples since ancient times, and is still often built now. | A traditional wall of packed earth held by a wooden framework on a stone foundation. | 围栏是为了防止敌人与动物的入侵，且具有遮蔽效果的防卫工事。<br>  筑地塀是以石垣为基底，搭上梁柱、骨架后，填入混凝土而成。自古以来常用于日本朝廷及寺院建设，即使到了现代也常被建筑。<br> |  |
| `WA_Wall_White.label` | Makabe(White) | Makabe(White) | 真壁(白) |  |
| `WA_Wall_White.description` | Creating traditional Japanese walls is called Makabe.<br>Makabe has a structure in which pillars and beams are exposed in the room, and it is said that durability is excellent because moisture easily escapes from the inside of the wall. | A traditional Japanese wall with exposed posts and beams. Its structure allows moisture to escape, helping the wall last. | 真壁，是以日本传统工法制成的墙壁。露出梁柱的构造使墙内的湿气容易发散，这项特征有助于增加墙壁的耐久性。 |  |
| `WA_Wall_Pink.label` | Makabe(Pink) | Makabe(Pink) | 真壁(桜) |  |
| `WA_Wall_Pink.description` | Creating traditional Japanese walls is called Makabe.<br>Makabe has a structure in which pillars and beams are exposed in the room, and it is said that durability is excellent because moisture easily escapes from the inside of the wall. | A traditional Japanese wall with exposed posts and beams. Its structure allows moisture to escape, helping the wall last. | 真壁，是以日本传统工法制成的墙壁。露出梁柱的构造使墙内的湿气容易发散，这项特征有助于增加墙壁的耐久性。 |  |

| `WA_Seat_Zabuton_Red.description` | The cushion contains cotton etc. as a cushioning material in a cloth bag and is used for sitting on tatami mats and floor.<br><br>Elasticity as much as Namba cushion is not required and the main purpose is to prevent body temperature from being deprived to the floor.<br><br>It was made around the Kamakura period, originally used as a symbol of power of authority and high priest.<br><br>It is said that middle people of the Edo period spread widely among ordinary people.<br><br>It is a design which was originally completed, and it is widely used even now. | A cloth cushion stuffed with cotton, used for sitting on tatami or the floor. It insulates the sitter from the cold floor. Once associated with dignitaries, it later became common in ordinary homes. | 座布团是在布袋中填入棉花等缓冲材料而制成的物品。在榻榻米及地板上坐下时使用。不像南蛮(葡萄牙)引进的垫子般具有高度弹力性，确保身体不会直接接触地面，而使体温散失才是主要目的。制作于鎌仓时期，原是作为掌权者及高僧的权力象征。<br>  直到江户时代中期才普及于庶民之间。原本的设计就已足够完善，至今亦广泛地被使用。 |  |
| `WA_Seat_Zabuton_Pink.description` | The cushion contains cotton etc. as a cushioning material in a cloth bag and is used for sitting on tatami mats and floor.<br><br>Elasticity as much as Namba cushion is not required and the main purpose is to prevent body temperature from being deprived to the floor.<br><br>It was made around the Kamakura period, originally used as a symbol of power of authority and high priest.<br><br>It is said that middle people of the Edo period spread widely among ordinary people.<br><br>It is a design which was originally completed, and it is widely used even now. | A cloth cushion stuffed with cotton, used for sitting on tatami or the floor. It insulates the sitter from the cold floor. Once associated with dignitaries, it later became common in ordinary homes. | 座布团是在布袋中填入棉花等缓冲材料而制成的物品。在榻榻米及地板上坐下时使用。不像南蛮(葡萄牙)引进的垫子般具有高度弹力性，确保身体不会直接接触地面，而使体温散失才是主要目的。制作于鎌仓时期，原是作为掌权者及高僧的权力象征。<br>  直到江户时代中期才普及于庶民之间。原本的设计就已足够完善，至今亦广泛地被使用。 |  |
| `WA_Seat_Zabuton_Yellow.description` | The cushion contains cotton etc. as a cushioning material in a cloth bag and is used for sitting on tatami mats and floor.<br><br>Elasticity as much as Namba cushion is not required and the main purpose is to prevent body temperature from being deprived to the floor.<br><br>It was made around the Kamakura period, originally used as a symbol of power of authority and high priest.<br><br>It is said that middle people of the Edo period spread widely among ordinary people.<br><br>It is a design which was originally completed, and it is widely used even now. | A cloth cushion stuffed with cotton, used for sitting on tatami or the floor. It insulates the sitter from the cold floor. | Key or path | Original | English | ChineseTraditional | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_Hediff_Metabolic.label` | ... A bit fat? | ... A little heavier? | …有點發福了？ |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Metabolic.comps.HediffComp_Discoverable.discoverLetterLabel` | {0} was fat | {0} gained weight | {0}稍微發福了 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Metabolic.comps.HediffComp_Discoverable.discoverLetterText` | {0} is now Metabolic! | {0} is now suffering the effects of too much sugar! | {0}變胖了！ |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Metabolic.stages.The_magical_power_of_sugar_gradually_corroves_you-0.label` | The magical power of sugar gradually corroves you. | The temptation of sugar is taking its toll. | 砂糖的魔力正逐漸侵蝕著你。 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Metabolic.stages.The_magical_power_of_sugar_gradually_corroves_you-1.label` | The magical power of sugar gradually corroves you. | The temptation of sugar is taking its toll. | 砂糖の魔力正逐漸侵蝕著你。 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Metabolic.stages.The_magical_power_of_sugar_gradually_corroves_you-2.label` | The magical power of sugar gradually corroves you. | The temptation of sugar is taking its toll. | 砂糖の魔力正逐漸侵蝕著你。 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_GreenTea.label` | Delicious Matcha | Delicious Matcha | 美味的抹茶 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_GreenTea.stages.I_drank_delicious_green_tea_and_the_spirit_was_sharpened.label` | I drank delicious green tea and the spirit was sharpened. | That delicious green tea cleared my mind. | 喝下美味的抹茶，使你感到精神澄清、思緒敏銳。 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Daifuku.label` | Delicious Ichigo Daifuku | Delicious Ichigo Daifuku | 美味的草莓大福 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Daifuku.stages.Fresh_and_fresh_strawberry_acidity_and_elegant_sweetness_of_strainer_Great.label` | Fresh and fresh strawberry acidity and elegant sweetness of strainer! Great! | Fresh strawberry, sweet bean paste and tender mochi: delicious! | 新鮮多汁的酸甜莓果，配上甘甜典雅的豆餡。堪稱絕品! |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Dango.label` | Delicious Mitarashi Dango | Delicious Mitarashi Dango | 御手洗糰子 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Dango.stages.The_compatibility_of_rich_sweet_sauce_is_great_with_rich_mochi_dumplings.label` | The compatibility of rich sweet sauce is great with rich mochi dumplings! | The sweet and savoury sauce goes perfectly with these chewy dumplings! | 軟Q帶嚼勁的糰子，淋上甜中有辣的濃厚醬汁。相性拔群! |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Geppei.label` | Delicious Geppei | Delicious Geppei | 美味的月餅 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Geppei.stages.I_was_impressed_with_the_crispy_feeling_of_bean_paste_and_walnut.label` | I was impressed with the crispy feeling of bean paste and walnut! | The sweet bean paste and crunchy walnuts were delightful! | 內餡的紮實綿密與脆口的胡桃之間巧妙結合，扣人心弦! |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Kashiwa.label` | Delicious Kashiwa Mochi | Delicious Kashiwa Mochi | 美味的柏餅 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Kashiwa.stages.The_sweetness_of_rice_and_the_smell_of_rice_are_deeply_flavored.label` | The sweetness of rice and the smell of rice are deeply flavored! | The sweetness of rice and the scent of oak leaves are a wonderful match! | 以槲葉的薰香包覆大米獨特的甘味，博大精深！ |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Kasuteira.label` | Delicious Kasuteira | Delicious Kasuteira | 美味的蜂蜜蛋糕 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Kasuteira.stages.The_fluffy_dough_and_the_texture_of_Zarame_feel_the_supreme_moment.label` | The fluffy dough and the texture of Zarame feel the supreme moment. | Fluffy cake and crunchy sugar crystals: a moment of bliss. | 軟綿蛋糕與砂糖粒的食感所交疊而成，那片刻的感動。 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Kintuba.label` | Delicious Kintuba | Delicious Kintuba | 美味的金鍔餅 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Kintuba.stages.Plenty_of_red_bean_paste_sweetness_is_moderately_sweet_and_you_will_even_remember_the_freshness.label` | Plenty of red bean paste sweetness is moderately sweet and you will even remember the freshness. | Generous red bean paste with a delicate sweetness. | 豐潤飽滿、甜度洽到好處的紅豆餡兒，在舌尖上留下一絲鮮甜。 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Nerikiri.label` | Delicious Nerikiri | Delicious Nerikiri | 美味的練切 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Nerikiri.stages.Multicolored_pastemaking_is_artistic.label` | Multicolored paste-making is artistic! | These colourful sweets are little works of art! | 五彩繽紛、如藝術般的糕點。别具匠心！ |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Sakura.label` | Delicious Sakura Mochi | Delicious Sakura Mochi | 美味的櫻餅 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Sakura.stages.An_elegant_scent_of_cherry_leaves_and_a_fabric_of_beautiful_color_make_spring_feel.label` | An elegant scent of cherry leaves and a fabric of beautiful color make spring feel. | The scent of cherry leaves and these delicate colours feel like spring. | 色彩柔美的餅衣，伴隨著櫻葉清幽的芬香，輕道著春天的到來。 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Uguisu.label` | Delicious Ugusisu Mochi | Delicious Uguisu Mochi | 美味的鶯餅 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Uguisu.stages.Vivid_wakakusa_color_is_very_compatible_with_soft_fertilizer.label` | Vivid wakakusa color is very compatible with soft fertilizer. | The fresh green colour suits this wonderfully soft mochi. | 柔軟的求肥與鮮亮的若草色十分相配。 |  |
| `WA_Hediff_Youkan.label` | Delicious Youkan | Delicious Youkan | 美味的羊羹 |  |

| `WA_MassCook_FineMeal.description` | Cook a somewhat complex meal from a combination of meat and plant ingredients. | Cook a somewhat complex meal from a combination of meat and plant ingredients. | 用肉和植物組合作為原料烹調一份相對複雜的料理。 |  |

Much of the ingredients are wasted. | Cook a very complex meal from a combination of meat and plant ingredients. Much of the ingredients are wasted. | 用肉和植物組合作為原料烹調一份非常複雜精緻的料理。絕大多數原料會被浪費掉。 |  |

| `WA_Recipe_GreenTea.label` | Make Green Tea | Make Green Tea | 點茶 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_GreenTea.description` | Dissolve tea leaves in powdered form in hot water and sprinkle tea. | Whisk powdered tea leaves into hot water. | 將茶葉細磨成末並溶於熱湯中，再以茶筅點茶。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_GreenTea.jobString` | Making Green Tea | Making Green Tea | 點製抹茶。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Daifuku.label` | Make Ichigo Daifuku | Make Ichigo Daifuku | 製作草苺大福 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Daifuku.description` | Make big strawberries wrapped in bean paste and rice cake. | Wrap strawberries in sweet bean paste and mochi. | 以內餡與餅衣，包覆大粒苺果。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Daifuku.jobString` | Making Ichigo Daifuku. | Making Ichigo Daifuku. | 製作草苺大福。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Dango.label` | Make Mitarashi dumplings | Make Mitarashi dumplings | 製作御手洗糰子 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Dango.description` | Make rice flour dumplings with a sweet amber colored sauce. | Make rice flour dumplings with a sweet amber colored sauce. | 糯米粉製成的糰子，淋上辛甜的琥珀色醬汁。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Dango.jobString` | Making Mitarashi dumplings. | Making Mitarashi dumplings. | 製作御手洗糰子。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Geppei.label` | Make Geppei | Make Geppei | 製作月餅 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Geppei.description` | Wrap the red bean paste in a dough and bake it. | Wrap sweet bean paste in pastry and bake it. | 以餅衣包上小紅豆餡烘烤製成。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Geppei.jobString` | Making Geppei. | Making Geppei. | 製作月餅。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kashiwa.label` | Make Kashiwa Mochi | Make Kashiwa Mochi | 製作柏餅 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kashiwa.description` | Wrapped in red bean paste with rice cake, wrapped in leaves of bamboo to make. | Wrap sweet bean paste in mochi, then wrap the cake in an oak leaf. | 以餅衣包裹小紅豆餡，再以覆上一枚槲葉。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kashiwa.jobString` | Making Kashiwa Mochi. | Making Kashiwa Mochi. | 製作柏餅。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kasuteira.label` | Make Kasuteira | Make Kasuteira | 製作蜂蜜蛋糕 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kasuteira.description` | Bubble the egg whites and mix the flour and sugar mixed dough. | Mix beaten eggs, flour and sugar into a light cake batter. | 攪拌蛋白直到起泡，與小麥粉、砂糖混成麵團烘焙。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kasuteira.jobString` | Making Kasuteira. | Making Kasuteira. | 製作蜂蜜蛋糕。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kintuba.label` | Make Kintuba | Make Kintuba | 製作金鍔餅 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kintuba.description` | Stir the grain bean paste with agar, bake each side with flour which is dissolved with water. | Set sweet bean paste with agar, then coat and cook each face in a thin batter. | 粒餡以寒天固定粒餡，在各面塗上溶於水的小麥粉後烘焙而成。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kintuba.jobString` | Making Kintuba. | Making Kintuba. | 製作金鍔餅。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Nerikiri.label` | Make Nerikiri | Make Nerikiri | 製作練切 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Nerikiri.description` | Add ingredients for the tie to the tinted bean paste, adjust and make it by making it. | Mix a binder into coloured bean paste and shape it into decorative sweets. | 揉進不同顏色的內餡中，添入增加黏性的食材。調整後反覆捏製而成。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Nerikiri.jobString` | Making Nerikiri. | Making Nerikiri. | 製作練切。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_SakuraNUguisu.label` | Make Sakura Mochi and Uguisu Mochi | Make Sakura Mochi and Uguisu Mochi | 製作櫻餅與鶯餅 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_SakuraNUguisu.description` | I make wrapped bean paste with two types of leather that colors spring. | Wrap sweet bean paste in two kinds of colourful spring mochi. | 取色於春天的兩種餅皮，包覆餡兒而成。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_SakuraNUguisu.jobString` | Making Sakura Mochi and Uguisu Mochi. | Making Sakura Mochi and Uguisu Mochi. | 製作櫻餅與鶯餅。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Youkan.label` | Make Youkan | Make Youkan | 製作羊羹 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Youkan.description` | Add the agar to the red bean paste and boil down to make it. | Cook sweet bean paste with agar to make a firm sweet. | 小紅豆餡加上寒天，熬煮後凝固而成。 |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Youkan.jobString` | Making Youkan. | Making Youkan. | 製作羊羹。 |  |
| `WA_Igusa2Medicine.label` | Purification Medicine | Prepare herbal medicine | 調藥 |  |
| `WA_Igusa2Medicine.description` | Purify rush as MedicineHerbals. | Process rush into herbal medicine. | 將燈心草煎製成藥。 |  |
| `WA_Igusa2Medicine.jobString` | Purify rush. | Preparing rush medicine. | 煎煮燈心草。 |  |
| `RPC_MakePureSilk.label` | Weaving silk | Weaving silk | 紡織蠶絲 |  |
| `RPC_MakePureSilk.description` | Weaving Pure Silk from cocoon. | Weave pure silk from silkworm cocoons. | 紡織蠶絲。 |  |
| `RPC_MakePureSilk.jobString` | Weaving silk. | Weaving silk. | 紡織蠶絲。 |  |
| `RPC_MakePaper.label` | Combing the Japanese paper | Make Japanese paper | 製作楮紙 |  |
| `RPC_MakePaper.description` | Combing the Japanese paper from Kouzo | Make Japanese paper from kozo bark fibres. | 透過流漉將楮皮製成和紙。 |  |
| `RPC_MakePaper.jobString` | Combing paper. | Making paper. | 製作楮紙。 |  |

## ChineseTraditional/DefInjected/ResearchProjectDef/Lang_WA-ResearchProject.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseTraditional | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_Research_Basic.label` | WA Basic | WA Basic | 和風技術_基礎 |  |
| `WA_Research_Basic.description` | Learn the basics of Japanese technology. We will be able to study various Japanese technologies. | Learn the foundations of Japanese techniques to unlock further research. | 學習基礎日本技術。解鎖各種日本技術研究。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Basic.label` | Building Basic | Basic architecture | 和風建築技術_基礎 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Basic.description` | Learn the basics of Japanese style building Technology.We will be able to build basic walls and fences | Learn basic Japanese building techniques to construct simple walls and fences. | 研究和風建築的基礎。能建築基本的牆壁與圍欄。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Advanced.label` | Building Advanced | Advanced architecture | 和風建築技術_應用 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Advanced.description` | Learn the application of Japanese style building Technology.We will be able to build various walls and fences. | Learn advanced Japanese building techniques to construct a wider range of walls and fences. | 研究和風建築的進階技術。以解鎖各式牆壁與圍欄。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Castle.label` | Building Castle | Fortified architecture | 和風築城技術 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Castle.description` | Learn the Japanese style casting technology.We will be able to build Robust building for defense. | Learn Japanese fortification techniques to build strong defensive structures. | 研究和風築城技術。適用建造於防衛的堅牢建築。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Garden.label` | Gardening | Gardening | 和風造園技術 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Garden.description` | Learn the Japanese style gardening technology.We will be able to build the landscape. | Learn Japanese gardening techniques to shape the landscape. | 研究和風造園技術。能解鎖用以美化景觀的建築。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Funiture.label` | Funiture | Furniture | 和風家具技術 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Funiture.description` | Learn the Japanese style funiture technology.We will be able to build various furniture. | Learn to make Japanese-style furniture. | 研究製作和風家具的方法。能製造多種家具。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Amusement.label` | Amusement | Amusement | 和風娛樂 |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Amusement.description` | Learn the Japanese style Entertainment technology.We will be able to build some entertainment supplies. | Learn to make equipment for traditional Japanese pastimes. | 學習娛樂技術。以製造多種娛樂用品。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Art.label` | Art | Art | 和風藝術品 |  |
| `WA_Research_Art.description` | Learn art.You will be able to make beautiful art pieces. | Learn to create beautiful works of art. | 學習藝術。能製作美麗的藝術品。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Farming.label` | Farming | Farming | 和風植物 |  |
| `WA_Research_Farming.description` | Learn cultivation of plants indispensable for Japanese style living. | Learn cultivation of plants indispensable for Japanese style living. | 學習如何栽培日常生活中不可缺乏的植物。 |  |
| `WA_Research_TeaTimeset.label` | Tea Time | Tea Time | 茶會 |  |
| `WA_Research_TeaTimeset.description` | Learn how to make tea.We will be able to make tea and kinds of tea confectionery. | Learn to prepare tea and traditional sweets to accompany it. | 學習如何泡茶。並能製作多種茶類與茶點。 |  |
| `WA_Research_Dressing.label` | Dress up | Dress up | 和服 |  |
| `WA_Research_Dressing.description` | Learn how to make daily clothing. | Learn how to make daily clothing. | 學習日常用服裝的製作手法。 |  |

It is a place of honor where a hanging scroll,<br>vase of arranged flowers or other ornaments are <br>displayed. | Tokonoma is a recessed alcove in a Japanese-style<br>room. ## ChineseTraditional/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-Amusement.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseTraditional | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_Koto.label` | Koto | Koto | 箏 |  |
| `WA_Koto.description` | Koto is a thirteen-stringed musical instrumentsimilar to a zither.The stirngs stretch over an oblong board. | A Japanese thirteen-stringed zither with strings stretched over a long wooden body. | 日本箏是有十三根琴弦的弦楽器。琴弦平行琴身。 |  |
| `WA_Karuta.label` | Karuta | Karuta | 歌牌 |  |
| `WA_Karuta.description` | Karuta is an indoor playground equipment that uses cards, mainly playing in the New Year. | A traditional Japanese card game, especially popular at New Year. | 歌牌是在正月時遊玩，利用卡牌進行的室內娛樂道具。 |  |
| `WA_ShellMatching.label` | ShellMatching | Shell matching | 貝合 |  |
| `WA_ShellMatching.description` | Shell Matching is a Japanese game transmitted from the Heian period. It was a play of aristocrats who competed for coloring of seashells, beauty of shape, rarity, or singing songs with the shellfish as a theme and competing for superiority or inferiority. | A traditional game of matching shells, recalling courtly pastimes in which their shapes, colours and associated poetry were admired. | 貝合是傳自於平安時代的日本遊戲。內容包括互相比較貝殻的色澤與外型，以及珍稀度。並以手中的貝殼為題材行歌頌詠，以分高下。是流行於貴族中的遊戲。 |  |
| `WA_Sugoroku.label` | Sugoroku | Sugoroku | 雙陸 |  |
| `WA_Sugoroku.description` | Sugoroku is a board game that advances the piece in the square according to the eyes that waved the dice and brings it closer. | A board game in which players move their pieces according to dice rolls. | 雙陸棋是種桌上遊戲。棋子的移動以擲骰子的點數決定，首位把所有馬移離棋盤的玩者可獲得勝利。 |  |
| `WA_Cassolette.label` | Cassolette | Incense burner | 香爐 |  |
| `WA_Cassolette.description` | An Cassolette is used to heat a perfume in a solid state and emit fragrance components.The nobility of the Heian period was a routine thing for men and women to be able to wear kimonos into incense burners and smell their bodies and clothes with scents. | A vessel for heating solid incense to release its scent. At the Heian court, incense was also used to perfume clothing. | 香爐是用於加熱固體香料，藉以散發薰香的容器。平安時代的貴族，無論男女皆會在穿著和服前會先過香，使薰芳環繞其身。 |  |

| `WA_Fusuma01_Akebono.description` | A sliding partition of paper or cloth stretched over a wooden frame. | `WA_Shoji01_Square.description` | A light wooden lattice covered with translucent paper. It admits soft light while screening the view. Its fragile paper is easily punctured. | A light wooden lattice covered with translucent paper. It admits soft light while screening the view. |---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_FarmPlant_Azuki.label` | Azuki | Azuki | 紅豆 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Azuki.description` | Tie the dark red beans to the sheath.<br>Beans are cooked or boiled sweet and then made into bean paste. | A plant bearing pods of dark red beans. These can be cooked as food or sweetened into bean paste. | 暗紅色的豆粒藏於豆莢中。<br>煮熟的紅豆能作為料理食用，加糖慢熬則能做成內餡。 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_TeaTree.label` | TeaTree | TeaTree | 茶樹 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_TeaTree.description` | It is said that cultivation began in China since BC, it is used not only for edible but also for appreciation.<br>The tea extracted from the leaves with water is loved around the world. | A shrub long cultivated for its leaves, which can be infused to make tea. | 據說在西元前就已在中國開始栽培，不單作為飲品，作為鑑賞植物亦有一分雅致。經水沖泡而製的茶受到世界各地喜愛。 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Igusa.label` | Rush | Rush | 燈心草 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Igusa.description` | It is an elongated plant inhabited by wetlands and large ones grow to about 1.6 m.<br>It is mainly used as a raw material for tatami mats, but it was used as a medicinal herbs in the past. | A tall wetland rush used primarily to make tatami mats, and historically in some herbal remedies. | 在溼地生長的細長植物，長者可達1.6米。常用於製作榻榻米，古時也常用作藥草。 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Kouzo.label` | Kouzo | Kouzo | 楮樹 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Kouzo.description` | In spring, light yellow green flowers bloom and red fruits in early summer.<br>It is used as a raw material for paper because it can take high quality fibers from the bark. | A paper-mulberry tree whose bark provides strong fibres for papermaking. | 楮樹會在春天綻放淡黃綠色的花，立夏時則會結下紅色的果實。能從樹皮抽取良好的纖維，常作為和紙的原料。 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Kuwa.label` | Kuwa | Kuwa | 桑樹 |  |
| `WA_FarmPlant_Kuwa.description` | Mulberry ties the purple of purplish red purple.<br>It is used as a tree for silkworms since ancient times, it is a plant indispensable for making silk. | A mulberry tree with purplish-red fruit. Its leaves feed silkworms, making it important for silk production. | 桑樹會結下紅紫的野苺。自古桑葉就常作為絲蠶的飼料，是製作細絹時不可或缺的植物。 |  |

## ChineseTraditional/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-FoldingScreen.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseTraditional | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_FoldingScreen.label` | Folding Screen | Folding Screen | 屏風 |  |
| `WA_FoldingScreen.description` | Byobu is a folding screen which consists of a wooden frame covered with thick Japanese paper or cloth, usually silk.<br>It is used as a decorative screen or room devider. | A folding screen of wooden frames covered with thick Japanese paper or cloth, often silk. It decorates a room or divides the space. | 屏風在西方又名折幕。以厚層和紙及絹絲覆蓋木架而成。屏风通常陳設於室內顯著位置，起到分隔、美化、擋风、協調等作用。 |  |

## ChineseTraditional/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-Food.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseTraditional | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_GreenTea.label` | Green Tea | Green Tea | 抹茶 |  |
| `WA_GreenTea.description` | Green Tea is a mixture of powdered tea leaves into hot water. | Green Tea is a mixture of powdered tea leaves into hot water. | 在日本，茶葉多被分類為綠茶，在日本時最為常見的飲品。茶會時則多製成抹茶，抹茶亦是綠茶的一種。將茶葉搗碎成末，加水攪拌而成。 |  |
| `WA_IchigoDaifuku.label` | Ichigo Daifuku | Ichigo Daifuku | 草苺大福 |  |
| `WA_IchigoDaifuku.description` | "Daifuku" is a rice cake stuffed with sweet filling like red bean paste.Daifuku literally means "great luck".<br>When eating, the fragrance of fresh strawberry spreads in the mouth, you can enjoy the sweetness of soft rice cake texture and elegant sweetness in exquisite balance. | A soft mochi filled with sweet red bean paste and a fresh strawberry. The fruit's tartness balances the sweet filling. | 大福一種是包著甜餡的糕餅。照其字義，有著象徵「幸運」的含意。嚼下一口只覺苺香鮮甜奔放，柔軟的餅皮與高雅的甜餡共譜出一章和諧。 |  |
| `WA_MitarashiDango.label` | Mitarashi Dango | Mitarashi Dango | 御手洗團子 |  |
| `WA_MitarashiDango.description` | "Mitarashi Dango" is a skewer dumpling with sugar and soy sauce.<br>The amber colored sweet taste is compatible with the rice flour dumplings, and the fragrant aroma caused by grilling with charcoal also enhances appetite. | Skewered rice-flour dumplings coated in a sweet soy-based sauce. Their grilled aroma is appetising. | 御手洗團子是淋上砂糖醬油的糰子串。琥珀色的辛甜淋醬與糯米糰子堪稱絕配，再經炭火烘烤後香氣四溢，令人食指大動。 |  |
| `WA_Geppei.label` | Geppei | Geppei | 月餅 |  |
| `WA_Geppei.description` | "Geppei" is an old sweet which is eaten in Mid-Autumn Festival in China.<br>It has a round shape that is regarded as a full moon, wrapped in a soft dough and baked red bean paste mixed with walnuts, pine nuts etc. in soft cloth. | A round mooncake filled with sweet bean paste and nuts, traditionally associated with the Mid-Autumn Festival. | 月餅常在中國的中秋節食用，是自古就有的甜點。取自滿月的外形，以柔軟的餅衣包覆含有胡桃及松果的紅豆餡，長時烘焙而成。 |  |
| `WA_KashiwaMochi.label` | Kashiwa Mochi | Kashiwa Mochi | 柏餅 |  |
| `WA_KashiwaMochi.description` | "Kashihachi" is wrapped in red bean paste with rice cake of rice flour. | A rice-flour mochi filled with sweet bean paste and wrapped in an oak leaf. | 柏餅是以上新粉製成的餅衣，填入紅豆餡，再用槲葉包裹而成的甜點。軟呼呼又帶嚼勁，咀嚼後，只覺糯米獨特的柔和甜香在口中綻放。 |  |
| `WA_Kasuteira.label` | Kasuteira | Kasuteira | 蜂蜜蛋糕 |  |
| `WA_Kasuteira.description` | "Kasuteira" is confections adopted from Portugal.<br>However, the actual situation is a completely different sweet that developed independently in Japan.<br>Kasuteira is made by burning a flour fabric mixed with plenty of sugar and eggs. | A light cake made from eggs, flour and sugar. Inspired by Portuguese baking, it developed its own distinctive form in Japan. | 蜂蜜蛋糕是傳自葡萄牙的南蠻糕點(日本古時稱葡萄牙為"南蠻")，在日本長年發展後，現已獨樹一幟。加入大量砂糖與蛋製成的糕身，口感剛柔並具，扎實且鬆軟。配上砂糖的香甜如同畫龍點睛，成就奢華的逸品。 |  |
| `WA_Kintuba.label` | Kintuba | Kintuba | 金鶚餅 |  |
| `WA_Kintuba.description` | "Kintuba" is a sweet made with red bean paste hardened with agar and baked with water flavored flour. | Sweet bean paste set with agar and cooked with a thin coating of batter. | 先以寒天固定紅豆餡，再塗上溶於水的小麥粉，烘製而成的糕點。色澤亮麗的粒餡，將紅豆的風味發揮得淋漓盡致，每一口都能將鮮甜的滋味烙印在腦海。 |  |
| `WA_Nerikiri.label` | Nerikiri | Nerikiri | 練切 |  |
| `WA_Nerikiri.description` | "Nerikiri" is a confectionary refined and adjusted by adding a connecting ingredient to white bean paste. | A decorative sweet shaped from white bean paste mixed with a binder, often coloured and formed into delicate patterns. | 練切是在白餡中添入黏性食材，經過調整後反覆捏製而成的生菓子。因其典雅的外型，與富含層次的風味而被歸類為「上生菓子」。練切的外型映照著四季的轉移，緊緊捉住觀賞者的心。 |  |
| `WA_SakuraNUguisu.label` | Sakura and Uguisu Mochi | Sakura and Uguisu Mochi | 櫻餅與鶯餅 |  |
| `WA_SakuraNUguisu.description` | It is a sweet imitating the color of cherry blossoms and the color of young plants. | Sweets evoking cherry blossoms and fresh spring shoots. | 以歌詠春季的櫻色及翠色為主題的糕點。兩者皆以米製餅皮包覆餡兒而成。櫻餅輕柔的櫻葉幽香，配上鶯餅的豆粉甜香，是幸福的芬芳。 |  |
| `WA_Youkan.label` | Youkan | Youkan | 羊羹 |  |
| `WA_Youkan.description` | "Youkan" is a Japanese sweet made by pouring azuki bean paste in mold and solidified with agar. | A Japanese sweet made from azuki bean paste, moulded and set with agar. | 羊羹是將紅豆餡放置於模子中，倒入寒天後凝固而成的和菓子。糖分高的羊羹在被切開時會流出砂糖，因此放置一段時間後會在外側形成一層白霜。<br>  白霜的顆粒感與羊羹內餡的軟嫩口感，譜成一章甜美的協奏曲。 |  |

| `WA_MFuton01m.description` | Futon is a thick bed quilt or mattress stuffed with cottonwool.A set of futon placed on Tatami mats is used as bedding.This futon is made for carrying and can be used like a bed roll. | A cotton-filled futon designed for carrying and use as a portable bedroll. | 布團是以填入厚重的棉花以製成的床墊及棉被套件。鋪在榻榻米上的一組布團能作為寢具使用。這個布團易於搬運，可做為睡袋使用。 |  |

| `WA_HFuton01m.description` | Futon is a thick bed quilt or mattress stuffed with cottonwool.A set of futon placed on Tatami mats is used as bedding.This futon is highly antimicrobial treated and can be used in medical practice. | A futon with an antimicrobial treatment, suitable for medical care. | 布團是以填入厚重的棉花以製成的床墊及棉被套件。鋪在榻榻米上的一組布團能作為寢具使用。這個布團經過抗菌處理，能在醫療手術時派上用場。 |  |

## ChineseTraditional/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-Glow.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseTraditional | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_Glow_01Toudai.label` | Cow dung lighthouse | Oil lamp stand | 牛糞燈台 |  |
| `WA_Glow_01Toudai.description` | The lighthouse lights the light with a light on the lamp core soaked in "kerosene" produced from the animal's oil.<br>It is said that it began to be used around the Heian era, and it is said that the aristocratic group used to love mainly.<br>Cow dung lighthouse has been called by this name because its base part resembles the form of cow dung. | An oil lamp with a wick resting in a shallow dish. Its traditional name refers to the shape of its base, which resembles cow dung. Such lamps were once used in aristocratic households. | 燈台，是將燈芯浸入由動物的脂肪製成的「燈油」中，用以點火照明的器具。據說從平安時代就有使用紀錄，主要是作為貴族圈的愛用品。其名源自於燈座形狀相似牛糞，故作此稱。 |  |
| `WA_Glow_02Gatou.label` | Gato | Gato | 瓦燈 |  |
| `WA_Glow_02Gatou.description` | Gato is also a lighting fixture made from "kerosene" made from animal fat.<br>It began to be used around the Muromachi period, and it circulated enough to be used as a light source for the common people in the Edo period.<br>It is called a tile light because it makes it by baking it using the same soil as the Kawara.<br>It is one of the popular reasons to be flame retardant materials. | A clay oil lamp made from material similar to roof tiles. Its fire-resistant construction helped it become a common household light. | 瓦燈是以動物脂肪所製成的「燈油」為燃料的照明器具。從室町時代就傳用至今，江戶時代時作為尋常百姓的生活光源而被廣泛利用。如同燒製瓦磚一般，透過窯燒黏土而成，其名亦源於此。不易燃的特性也利於人所用。 |  |
| `WA_Glow_03Andon.label` | Ariake lantern | Ariake lantern | 有明行燈 |  |
| `WA_Glow_03Andon.description` | Lighthouses and Gato were problematic in windshields, so lanterns wrapped in Japanese paper were invented.<br>Lanterns were originally used for carrying, but since it was "kerosene" made from animal oil, there is a difficulty in portability and it will be used for indoor stationary use.<br>Ariake lanterns can be illuminated brightly when used out from the box, and when used in a box it gained popularity as an excellent one that can be used as a bedtime light with suppressed brightness. | A paper-covered lantern that shelters its flame. Outside its box it gives a brighter light; inside, it becomes a subdued night light. | 因為燈台與瓦燈防風不佳，易被吹滅。以和紙框住火光的「行燈」因而問世。行燈本是作為行路時的手提式光源。又因以動物脂肪製成的「燈油」常於行進時液出而不易攜帶，現在多擺設於室内使用。有明行燈可從箱中取出，增加室內照明效果。就寢時則能放入箱中，降低明亮度。因其優秀的機能性而頗具人氣。 |  |
| `WA_Glow_04Bonbori.label` | Bonbori | Bonbori | 雪洞 |  |
| `WA_Glow_04Bonbori.description` | The weak point of kerosene which is liquid is easy to spill and lack portability.<br>So lighting using candles that will not spill will be accepted.<br>Snow cave is made by covering a candle on a candlestick with paper or cloth, and something of various shapes such as square, hexagon, octagon, round shape etc are made.<br>Even now there are habits left to decorate Bonbori during events such as dolls' festival. | A lantern made by covering a frame with paper or cloth around a candle. Bonbori still decorate traditional celebrations such as the Doll Festival. | 液態燈油液在行路攜帶時有著容易濺出的缺點。基於這點，固態蠟燭開始廣用於照明燈具。雪洞是將蠟燭放置在燭台上，再以和紙或布來包覆製成的物品。能製成四角形、六角形、八角形、圓型等多種造型。在現今，雛祭等節日時依然會以雪洞作為裝飾使用。 |  |
| `WA_Glow_05Chouchin.label` | Chochin | Chochin | 提燈 |  |
| `WA_Glow_05Chouchin.description` | Chochin is also lighting with a candle, which is very light because it covers the body made of bamboo chicken and Japanese paper.<br>It became a structure that can be folded up, and because of its portability it became an alternative to lighting for portability.<br>It is said that it was made in the Azuchi-Momoyama period and spread in the Edo period.<br>Furthermore, even now the contents are different and loved and it continues to be used. | A lightweight paper lantern stretched over a folding bamboo frame. Portable and compact, it has long been used in streets and at festivals. | 提燈亦是利用蠟燭的照明用具。用竹板與和紙製成的骨架十分輕便，是其一大特徵。加上可摺疊的機能，使其攜帶性優於行燈而逐漸代之。最早始於安土桃山時代，在江戸時代開始普及。即使是現今，亦能放入多不同光源而被持續使用。 |  |
| `WA_Glow_06Bonfire.label` | Bonfire | Bonfire | 篝火 |  |
| `WA_Glow_06Bonfire.description` | Bonfire are one of the lighting fixtures since ancient times, mainly installed outdoors.<br>Put the fire in a steel basket with fuel like pine as a fuel.<br>In addition to being used for outdoor events such as martial forces and festivals, it also fulfills the role of a fishing light on fishing boats.<br>Even now it is used at night festival events and cormorants. | An outdoor fire held in a metal basket. It lights gatherings, festivals and some fishing boats. | 篝火は是以古以來就有的照明用具。主要放置於屋外，以松木等富含樹脂的樹木作為燃料，放入鐵製籠座中點火使用。在排演軍陣、慶祝節日等屋外活動時常被使用，此外，在漁船上亦能作為聚魚燈。現今則能在夜間祭典上，或是鸕鶿捕魚時派上用場。 |  |
| `WA_Glow_07LongBrazier.label` | Brazier | Brazier | 火鉢 |  |
| `WA_Glow_07LongBrazier.description` | Brazier is half-filled with burning charcoal.<br>It is one of the traditional heating devices.<br>In addition to heating, you can boil tea or tobacco pipe. | A vessel partly filled with glowing charcoal. It provides warmth and can heat tea or light a pipe. | 火鉢是在陶器中，放入半分滿的木炭，燃燒以提升室內溫度。火鉢是日本傳統的暖房器具之一。 |  |

## ChineseTraditional/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-Irori.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseTraditional | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_Irori.label` | Irori | Irori | 圍爐裏 |  |
| `WA_Irori.description` | It is a type of furnace permanently installed indoors.<br>It is a corner established in traditional Japanese houses to cut off the floor square, open ash, spread firewood and charcoal fire.<br>It is of the type put in the middle of a large room and has various roles such as heating, cooking, lighting, drying. | A sunken hearth in a square opening in the floor, filled with ash, wood and charcoal. It provides heating, cooking, light and a place to dry things. | 一種設置於屋内的永久性家俱。傳統的日式住宅會在地板挖開一塊四方形空間並鋪上灰燼，用來燃燒木炭或柴火。設置在主廳房的圍爐裏，具有暖房、調理、照明、乾燥等多種用途。 |  |
| `WA_Chagama.label` | Irori(TeaSet) | Irori(TeaSet) | 茶室用圍爐裏 |  |
| `WA_Chagama.description` | It is a type of furnace permanently installed indoors.<br>It is a corner established in traditional Japanese houses to cut off the floor square, open ash, spread firewood and charcoal fire.<br>Most of the hearths used in the tea room are sized for one tatami mat, mainly used for boiling water. | A sunken hearth set into the floor of a tea room, primarily used to heat water for tea. | 一種設置於屋内的永久性家俱。傳統的日式住宅會在地板挖開一塊四方形空間並鋪上灰燼，用來燃燒木炭或柴火。<br>用於茶室的圍爐裏通常占一張榻榻米大小，主要用來煮開水。 |  |

## ChineseTraditional/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-RawPlant.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseTraditional | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_Azuki.label` | Azuki | Azuki | 小豆 |  |
| `WA_Azuki.description` | Raw Red beens. | Raw red beans. | 未煮過的小豆。 |  |
| `WA_TeaLeaf.label` | TeaLeaf | Tea leaves | 茶葉 |  |
| `WA_TeaLeaf.description` | Raw Tea Leaf. | Fresh tea leaves. | 生茶葉。 |  |
| `WA_Igusa.label` | Rush | Rush | 燈心草 |  |
| `WA_Igusa.description` | Rush is mainly used as a raw material for tatami mats, but it was used as a medicinal herbs in the past. | Rush is mainly used as a raw material for tatami mats, but it was used as a medicinal herbs in the past. | 燈心草主要用於製作榻榻米，在古時亦作為藥草使用。 |  |
| `RPC_Kouzo.label` | Kouzo | Kouzo | 楮樹皮 |  |
| `RPC_Kouzo.description` | It is a bundle of bark which took from Kouzo Tree.<br>Fibers of Kouzo bark are strongly intertwined long.<br>Paper made from Kouzo is strong and lasts long. | A bundle of paper-mulberry bark. Its long, intertwined fibres make strong, lasting paper. | 將樹皮剝開後捆成一束的物品。楮樹皮的纖維具有細長又相互糾結的特性，適用於製作高韌性而不易破的和紙。 |  |
| `RPC_Cocoon.label` | Cocoon | Cocoon | 繭 |  |
| `RPC_Cocoon.description` | It is the cocoon made by silkworm larvae.<br>Weaving the thread by spinning it will become the finest textile. | A silkworm cocoon. Its thread can be reeled off to produce fine silk fabric. | 絲蠶的幼蟲製成的繭。經由紡絲編織便可成為最上等的布帛。 |  |

## ChineseTraditional/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-Wall.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseTraditional | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_Wall_Kawara.label` | Rampart | Rampart | 城壁 |  |
| `WA_Wall_Kawara.description` | It is a type of painted wall.<br>The roof tiles(Kawara) roofed on the plastics wall also combine rain removal and decoration.<br>At that time the tiles were expensive, mainly used for the outer walls of samurai residences. | A plastered wall capped with roof tiles for weather protection and decoration. Such walls once surrounded the residences of samurai. | 一種塗牆，塗牆是使用水、土、植物等天然素材作為原料的牆壁。鋪上漆喰壁的屋瓦既能防雨，亦帶有裝飾效果。在當時的瓦磚價格不斐，主要用來建築武家房屋的外牆。 |  |
| `WA_Wall_Demado.label` | Rampart(bay window) | Rampart(bay window) | 城壁(凸窗) |  |
| `WA_Wall_Demado.description` | It is one with a bay window attached to the wall with tiles.<br>The bay window functioned as a surveillance window besides the role of giving aesthetic appearance during normal times.<br>In addition, it fulfills the role of firearms and stone drops at wartime. | A tiled wall with a projecting window for watching the surroundings and supporting its defenders. | 在鋪有屋瓦的牆壁上設置凸窗。凸窗平時除了美觀效果，亦能用於監視外側。戰鬥時則能作為銃眼、投石處來迎敵。 |  |
| `WA_Wall_StoneWall.label` | Stone Wall | Stone Wall | 石垣 |  |
| `WA_Wall_StoneWall.description` | Ishigaki is a wall created by assembling stones and rocks, and is mainly used as a defense such as a castle.<br>Initially it was just a stack of natural stones, but as the era has progressed, things that increased the defensibility by loading stones machined without gaps became mainstream. | Ishigaki is a wall created by assembling stones and rocks, and is mainly used as a defense such as a castle.<br>Initially it was just a stack of natural stones, but as the era has progressed, things that increased the defensibility by loading stones machined without gaps became mainstream. | 石垣是由石頭或岩石堆砌而成的牆壁，只要用來建造城塞的防衛牆。在早期只是將普通的石頭堆疊起來而已。隨著時代演進，將加工後的石塊無間隙地堆砌成牆，以提高防禦性能的形式逐漸成為主流。 |  |
| `WA_Wall_WaterMoats.label` | Water Moats | Water Moats | 城壕 |  |
| `WA_Wall_WaterMoats.description` | The moat was dug around the castle and temple from the ancient times to prevent invasion of enemies and animals.<br>It has width and depth that can not easily be exceeded and it functions as an important defense facility.<br>Those water-trenched to the moat are called Mizuhori in particular, and have been incorporated into many castles since the modern era. | A wide, deep ditch dug around a fortification to slow intruders. Filled with water, it is a serious obstacle for attackers. | 是古時由人工挖鑿，環繞整座城、宮城、寺院等主要建築的壕溝，具有防禦作用，可防止敵人或動物入侵。具有不易越過的深度及寬度，能作為重要的防衛建設使用。<br>  掘溝放水而成的城壕又稱護城河，近代以後有多座城堡採用這種設計。 |  |
| `WA_Wall_DryMoats.label` | Dry Moats | Dry Moats | 乾壕 |  |
| `WA_Wall_DryMoats.description` | The moat was dug around the castle and temple from the ancient times to prevent invasion of enemies and animals.<br>It has width and depth that can not easily be exceeded and it functions as an important defense facility.<br>Those water-trenched to the moat are called Mizuhori in particular, and have been incorporated into many castles since the modern era. | A wide, deep ditch dug around a fortification to slow intruders. It stays dry and is an obstacle for attackers. | 是古時由人工挖鑿，環繞整座城、宮城、寺院等主要建築的壕溝，具有防禦作用，可防止敵人或動物入侵。具有不易越過的深度及寬度，能作為重要的防衛建設使用。<br>  掘溝而不引河而成的城壕又稱乾壕，中世紀時的壕溝多為乾壕。空壕能防止敵人透過船隻入侵，也沒有護城河需顧慮到河面結冰的問題。 |  |
| `WA_Step.label` | Step | Step | 階梯 |  |
| `WA_Step.description` | The staircase is a structure to move to a place with a height difference.<br>It is divided into several horizontal stages with a height that can be raised and lowered by human feet.<br>Rimworld without the notion of step difference has no meaning other than appearance. | A decorative step suggesting a change in floor level. RimWorld does not model such changes, so its role here is purely visual. | 樓梯是常用來聯繫具有高低差的兩地時所採用的交通建設。以人類的雙腳能在其上升降的多個水平段落來分割垂直高度。在邊緣世界這款沒有段差概念的遊戲，除了當裝飾以外並不具有其他意義。 |  |
| `WA_Gate_CastleGate.label` | Castle Gate | Castle Gate | 城門 |  |
| `WA_Gate_CastleGate.description` | The castle gate was one of the few places to enter the castle and it was one of the most important defense lines at wartime.<br>For that reason, it was easy to make a robust structure that can not be withdrawn, and a lot of ingenious efforts were made for interception. | A strong, fortified castle entrance designed to aid its defenders. | 城門是能進入城內的少數區域，也是戰時最為重要的防線之一。因此，<br>  城門大多設計成無法輕易通過的堅固設施，凝聚了工匠的無數心力，以利於守城方迎擊敵人。 |  |
| `WA_Gate_IronGate.label` | Castle Door | Castle Door | 鉄扉 |  |
| `WA_Gate_IronGate.description` | The castle gate was one of the few places to enter the castle and it was one of the most important defense lines at wartime.<br>Some of the gate is made of heavy iron, and even just opening it required a very large effort. | A heavy door protecting a castle entrance, sometimes reinforced with iron. | 城門是能進入城內的少數區域，也是戰時最為重要的防線之一。<br>  門扉的部分由沉重的鐵所製，僅是要打開它就得耗上巨大勞力。 |  |
| `WA_Fence_Yamatobei.label` | Yamato Fence | Yamato Fence | 大和塀 |  |
| `WA_Fence_Yamatobei.description` | The fence is a workpiece that has the effect of preventing invasion of people and animals and blindfolding.<br>Yamato-Fence is a fence stretched from the front and back with alternating plates one by one.<br>I open a gap by passing a crosspiece between the boards.<br>By doing so, it is a simple fence that blocks the line of sight and also passes the wind. | A fence with boards alternating on either side of its crosspieces. It blocks the view while allowing air to pass. | 圍欄是為了防止敵人與動物的入侵，且具有遮蔽效果的防衛工事。<br>大和塀是在一層木製柵欄，在裏外具有數個重合部分。木板間能透過短桟開啟縫隙。藉此形成能遮蔽視線，又能通風的簡易屏障。 |  |
| `WA_Fence_Sukibei.label` | Suki Fence | Suki Fence | 透塀 |  |
| `WA_Fence_Sukibei.description` | The fence is a workpiece that has the effect of preventing invasion of people and animals and blindfolding.<br>Suki-Fence is a covered fence, so that the interior can be seen through by placing a lattice in the middle.<br>It is mainly used in shrines and temples. | A covered fence with a central lattice that allows a partial view inside. Often used at shrines and temples. | 圍欄是為了防止敵人與動物的入侵，且具有遮蔽效果的防衛工事。<br>透塀是搭蓋了屋頂的圍欄，中間鑿了數個方格，能從方格看到內部。主要使用於神社及寺廟。 |  |
| `WA_Fence_Tuijibei.label` | Tsuiji Fence | Tsuiji Fence | 築地塀 |  |
| `WA_Fence_Tuijibei.description` | The fence is a workpiece that has the effect of preventing invasion of people and animals and blindfolding.<br>Tsuiji-Fence sets up a framework by setting up a pillar on the foundation of stone wall, pouring and kneading the kneading soil.<br>It has been used in public places and temples since ancient times, and is still often built now. | A traditional wall of packed earth held by a wooden framework on a stone foundation. | 圍欄是為了防止敵人與動物的入侵，且具有遮蔽效果的防衛工事。<br>築地塀是以石垣為基礎，搭建柱子及骨架後，填入混凝土而成的屏障。自古以來就常用於日本朝廷及寺院等建設。即使是現代依然有數件建案。 |  |
| `WA_Wall_White.label` | Makabe(White) | Makabe(White) | 真壁(白) |  |
| `WA_Wall_White.description` | Creating traditional Japanese walls is called Makabe.<br>Makabe has a structure in which pillars and beams are exposed in the room, and it is said that durability is excellent because moisture easily escapes from the inside of the wall. | A traditional Japanese wall with exposed posts and beams. Its structure allows moisture to escape, helping the wall last. | 真壁，是以日本傳統工法製成的牆壁。露出梁柱的構造使牆內的濕氣容易發散，這項特徵有助於增加牆壁的耐久性。 |  |
| `WA_Wall_Pink.label` | Makabe(Pink) | Makabe(Pink) | 真壁(桜) |  |
| `WA_Wall_Pink.description` | Creating traditional Japanese walls is called Makabe.<br>Makabe has a structure in which pillars and beams are exposed in the room, and it is said that durability is excellent because moisture easily escapes from the inside of the wall. | A traditional Japanese wall with exposed posts and beams. Its structure allows moisture to escape, helping the wall last. | 真壁，是以日本傳統工法製成的牆壁。露出梁柱的構造使牆內的濕氣容易發散，這項特徵有助於增加牆壁的耐久性。 |  |

## ChineseTraditional/DefInjected/ThingDef/Lang_WA-Zabuton.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | ChineseTraditional | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_Seat_Zabuton_Red.label` | Zabuton(Red) | Zabuton(Red) | 座布団(茜) |  |
| `WA_Seat_Zabuton_Pink.label` | Zabuton(Pink) | Zabuton(Pink) | 座布団(紅梅) |  |
| `WA_Seat_Zabuton_Yellow.label` | Zabuton(Yellow) | Zabuton(Yellow) | 座布団(山吹) |  |
| `WA_Seat_Zabuton_Green.label` | Zabuton(Green) | Zabuton(Green) | 座布団(萌黄) |  |
| `WA_Seat_Zabuton_Blue.label` | Zabuton(Blue) | Zabuton(Blue) | 座布団(瑠璃) |  |
| `WA_Seat_Zabuton_Purple.label` | Zabuton(Purple) | Zabuton(Purple) | 座布団(紫苑) |  |
| `WA_Seat_Zabuton_Black.label` | Zabuton(Black) | Zabuton(Black) | 座布団(鳩羽鼠) |  |
| `WA_Seat_Zabuton_White.label` | Zabuton(White) | Zabuton(White) | 座布団(白) |  |
| `WA_Seat_Zabuton_Red.description` | The cushion contains cotton etc. as a cushioning material in a cloth bag and is used for sitting on tatami mats and floor.<br><br>Elasticity as much as Namba cushion is not required and the main purpose is to prevent body temperature from being deprived to the floor.<br><br>It was made around the Kamakura period, originally used as a symbol of power of authority and high priest.<br><br>It is said that middle people of the Edo period spread widely among ordinary people.<br><br>It is a design which was originally completed, and it is widely used even now. | A cloth cushion stuffed with cotton, used for sitting on tatami or the floor. It insulates the sitter from the cold floor. Once associated with dignitaries, it later became common in ordinary homes. | 座布團是在布袋中填入棉花等緩衝材料而製成的物品。在榻榻米及地板上坐下時使用。不像南蠻(葡萄牙)引進的墊子般具有高度彈力性，確保身體不會直接接觸地面，而使體溫散失才是主要目的。製作於鎌倉時期，原是作為掌權者及高僧的權力象徵。<br>  直到江戶時代中期才普及於庶民之間。原本的設計就已足夠完善，至今亦廣泛地被使用。 |  |

| `WA_Recipe_Dango.description` | Make rice flour dumplings with a sweet amber colored sauce. | Make rice flour dumplings with a sweet amber colored sauce. | Préparer des boulettes de farine de riz nappées d'une sauce ambrée sucrée-salée. |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Dango.jobString` | Making Mitarashi dumplings. | Making Mitarashi dumplings. | Prépare des mitarashi dango. |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Geppei.label` | Make Geppei | Make Geppei | Préparer des geppei |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Geppei.description` | Wrap the red bean paste in a dough and bake it. | Wrap sweet bean paste in pastry and bake it. | Envelopper de la pâte de haricots rouges dans une pâte à gâteau et la cuire. |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Geppei.jobString` | Making Geppei. | Making Geppei. | Prépare des geppei. |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kashiwa.label` | Make Kashiwa Mochi | Make Kashiwa Mochi | Préparer des kashiwa mochi |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kashiwa.description` | Wrapped in red bean paste with rice cake, wrapped in leaves of bamboo to make. | Wrap sweet bean paste in mochi, then wrap the cake in an oak leaf. | Envelopper de la pâte de haricots rouges dans du mochi, puis dans une feuille de chêne. |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kashiwa.jobString` | Making Kashiwa Mochi. | Making Kashiwa Mochi. | Prépare des kashiwa mochi. |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kasuteira.label` | Make Kasuteira | Make Kasuteira | Préparer du kasutera |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kasuteira.description` | Bubble the egg whites and mix the flour and sugar mixed dough. | Mix beaten eggs, flour and sugar into a light cake batter. | Mélanger des œufs battus, de la farine et du sucre pour obtenir un gâteau moelleux. |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kasuteira.jobString` | Making Kasuteira. | Making Kasuteira. | Prépare du kasutera. |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kintuba.label` | Make Kintuba | Make Kintuba | Préparer des kintsuba |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kintuba.description` | Stir the grain bean paste with agar, bake each side with flour which is dissolved with water. | Set sweet bean paste with agar, then coat and cook each face in a thin batter. | Faire prendre la pâte de haricots rouges avec de l'agar-agar, puis cuire chaque face sous une fine couche de pâte. |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Kintuba.jobString` | Making Kintuba. | Making Kintuba. | Prépare des kintsuba. |  |

| `WA_Recipe_Nerikiri.description` | Add ingredients for the tie to the tinted bean paste, adjust and make it by making it. | Mix a binder into coloured bean paste and shape it into decorative sweets. | Mélanger un liant à de la pâte de haricots colorée, puis façonner des pâtisseries décoratives. |  |
| `WA_Recipe_Nerikiri.jobString` | Making Nerikiri. | Making Nerikiri. | Prépare des nerikiri. |  |

| `WA_Recipe_SakuraNUguisu.jobString` | Making Sakura Mochi and Uguisu Mochi. | Making Sakura Mochi and Uguisu Mochi. | Prépare des sakura et uguisu mochi. |  |

| `WA_Recipe_Youkan.jobString` | Making Youkan. | Making Youkan. | Prépare du yōkan. |  |

## French/DefInjected/ResearchProjectDef/WA.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | French | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WA_Research_Basic.label` | WA Basic | WA Basic | Fondements japonais |  |
| `WA_Research_Basic.description` | Learn the basics of Japanese technology. We will be able to study various Japanese technologies. | Learn the foundations of Japanese techniques to unlock further research. | Apprendre les bases des techniques japonaises pour accéder à leurs différents domaines. |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Basic.label` | Building Basic | Basic architecture | Architecture élémentaire |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Basic.description` | Learn the basics of Japanese style building Technology.We will be able to build basic walls and fences | Learn basic Japanese building techniques to construct simple walls and fences. | Apprendre les bases de l'architecture japonaise pour construire des murs et clôtures simples. |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Advanced.label` | Building Advanced | Advanced architecture | Architecture avancée |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Advanced.description` | Learn the application of Japanese style building Technology.We will be able to build various walls and fences. | Learn advanced Japanese building techniques to construct a wider range of walls and fences. | Approfondir les techniques japonaises pour construire des murs et clôtures variés. |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Castle.label` | Building Castle | Fortified architecture | Architecture fortifiée |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Castle.description` | Learn the Japanese style casting technology.We will be able to build Robust building for defense. | Learn Japanese fortification techniques to build strong defensive structures. | Apprendre les techniques japonaises de fortification pour construire de solides défenses. |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Garden.label` | Gardening | Gardening | Jardins |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Garden.description` | Learn the Japanese style gardening technology.We will be able to build the landscape. | Learn Japanese gardening techniques to shape the landscape. | Apprendre les techniques de jardinage japonaises pour aménager le paysage. |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Funiture.label` | Funiture | Furniture | Mobilier |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Funiture.description` | Learn the Japanese style funiture technology.We will be able to build various furniture. | Learn to make Japanese-style furniture. | Apprendre à fabriquer divers meubles de style japonais. |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Amusement.label` | Amusement | Amusement | Loisirs |  |
| `WA_Research_Building_Amusement.description` | Learn the Japanese style Entertainment technology.We will be able to build some entertainment supplies. | Learn to make equipment for traditional Japanese pastimes. | Apprendre à fabriquer des objets de loisirs traditionnels japonais. |  |
| `WA_Research_Art.label` | Art | Art | Art |  |
| `WA_Research_Art.description` | Learn art.You will be able to make beautiful art pieces. | Learn to create beautiful works of art. | Apprendre à réaliser de belles œuvres d'art. |  |
| `WA_Research_Farming.label` | Farming | Farming | Agriculture |  |
| `WA_Research_Farming.description` | Learn cultivation of plants indispensable for Japanese style living. | Learn cultivation of plants indispensable for Japanese style living. | Apprendre à cultiver les plantes indispensables à la vie japonaise. |  |
| `WA_Research_TeaTimeset.label` | Tea Time | Tea Time | L'heure du thé |  |
| `WA_Research_TeaTimeset.description` | Learn how to make tea.We will be able to make tea and kinds of tea confectionery. | Learn to prepare tea and traditional sweets to accompany it. | Apprendre à préparer le thé et les pâtisseries qui l'accompagnent. |  |
| `WA_Research_Dressing.label` | Dress up | Dress up | Habillement |  |
| `WA_Research_Dressing.description` | Learn how to make daily clothing. | Learn how to make daily clothing. | Apprendre à confectionner des vêtements du quotidien. |  |
